shrink-0\"></div><p class=\"text-[10px] text-white/90 leading-normal whitespace-pre-line flex-1\">The daily drawdown sets the maximum amount your account can lose in a single trading day.\nAt the start of each day, the higher value between your balance and equity becomes the baseline. Your equity must not fall below the daily limit, which is calculated as a percentage of that starting value.\n\nExample (different scenario):\nIf Day 2 begins with a balance of $82,000 and equity of $84,500, and your daily limit is 3%, then:\n3% of $84,500 = $2,535\nThis means on Day 2, your equity must stay above $81,965.\n</p><div class=\"w-4 shrink-0\"></div></div><div class=\"h-3 w-full shrink-0\"></div></div></div></div><div class=\"h-[48px] flex items-center border-b border-white/10 last:border-0 relative\"><div class=\"w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-white/20 flex items-center justify-center border border-white/30\"><svg xmlns=\"http://www.w3.org/2000/svg\" width=\"10\" height=\"10\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" class=\"lucide lucide-check text-white\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"M20 6 9 17l-5-5\"></path></svg></div><div class=\"w-3 shrink-0\"></div><span class=\"text-sm font-semibold text-white\">Maximum Drawdown</span><div class=\"w-2 shrink-0\"></div><div class=\"relative group\"><svg xmlns=\"http://www.w3.org/2000/svg\" width=\"14\" height=\"14\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" class=\"lucide lucide-info text-white/40 group-hover:text-white cursor-help\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><circle cx=\"12\" cy=\"12\" r=\"10\"></circle><path d=\"M12 16v-4\"></path><path d=\"M12 8h.01\"></path></svg><div class=\"absolute right-[calc(100%+12px)] top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 hidden group-hover:flex flex-col z-50 w-64 bg-[#02051B] border border-white/30 rounded-xl shadow-2xl pointer-events-none\"><div class=\"h-3 w-full shrink-0\"></div><div class=\"flex w-full\"><div class=\"w-4 shrink-0\"></div><p class=\"text-[10px] text-white/90 leading-normal whitespace-pre-line flex-1\">The maximum drawdown rule protects the total account. It represents the lowest point your balance or equity is allowed to reach during the entire evaluation.\nThis limit is calculated as a fixed percentage of your starting account size. \n\nExample :\nIf your account begins at $100,000 and the max drawdown is 6%, your account must never fall below $94,000, regardless of whether the loss is from open trades or closed trades.\n\nNote:\nGen Z Instant and Standard 1 Step plans follows a maximum trailing drawdown</p><div class=\"w-4 shrink-0\"></div></div><div class=\"h-3 w-full shrink-0\"></div></div></div></div><div class=\"h-[48px] flex items-center
